## Combo 276-1775-2968
Cards: Toralf, God of Fury // Toralf's Hammer + Ajani's Presence + Angrath's Marauders
Color identity: RW | Bracket: Exhibition | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal | Mana needed: {2}{W}{W}
Produces: Infinite damage to one opponent
Start: Toralf, God of Fury // Toralf's Hammer (battlefield); Ajani's Presence (hand); Angrath's Marauders (battlefield)
Prerequisites: An opponent controls two or more creatures that can be targeted by spells and abilities you control.
  You have a way to deal noncombat damage to one of the indestructible creatures such that the excess damage doubled will deal excess damage to the second indestructible creature.
Steps: Cast Ajani's Presence by paying {2}{W}{W} total, giving two creatures an opponent controls indestructible until end of turn.
  Deal noncombat damage to one of the indestructible creatures, dealing an increased amount of damage instead due to Angrath's Marauders.
  Toralf triggers, dealing an amount of damage over the excess damage to the second indestructible creature due to Angrath's Marauders.
  Toralf triggers, dealing an amount of damage over the excess damage to the first indestructible creature due to Angrath's Marauders.
  Repeat from step 3 an arbitrarily large number of times.
  Resolve a Toralf trigger, dealing infinite damage to any target.
Notes: An example of the pre-requisite: If you give two creatures an opponent controls with toughness 2 and 3 indestructible, you would need to start the loop by dealing 4 noncombat damage to the creature with toughness equal to 2. This will deal 2 excess damage, which will be amplified to 4, thus dealing excess damage to the second creature and continuing the loop. It is recommended to start by giving the two smallest creatures possible indestructible and to target the smaller of the two creatures first.

  Resolve the Mysterious Limousine trigger, exiling Fear of Missing Out or Godo from the battlefield, and then returning Fear of Missing Out or Godo from exile to the battlefield.
  Repeat each combat phase.

  Breath of Fury triggers, causing you to sacrifice the creature it is attached to, then attach it to another Angel you control, untap all creatures you control, and get an additional combat phase after this one.
  When the Angel dies, Bishop of Wings' second ability triggers, creating a 4/4 Angel creature token due to Divine Visitation.
  When the Angel enters, Bishop of Wings' first ability triggers, causing you to gain 4 life.
  Repeat each combat phase.

Steps: Declare the creature with Breath of Fury attached and Brimaz as attackers.
  Brimaz triggers, creating a creature token.
  Deal combat damage using the creature that has Breath of Fury attached. 
  Breath of Fury triggers, causing you to sacrifice the creature it is attached to, then attach Breath of Fury to the creature token, then causing you to untap all creatures you control and to get an additional combat phase after this one.
  Repeat each combat phase.

## Combo 3259-4885-7917--185
Cards: Breath of Fury + Seluma, Light of Aysen + Tuktuk Rubblefort
Color identity: RW | Bracket: Banned | Popularity: 0 | Commander: not legal
Produces: Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite combat damage; Infinite combat phases; Infinite death triggers; Infinite mana creatures you control can produce; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite untap of creatures you control
Requires: 2x Angel Creature (battlefield, graveyard)
Start: Breath of Fury (battlefield: attached to the other Angel on the battlefield); Seluma, Light of Aysen (battlefield); Tuktuk Rubblefort (battlefield)
Prerequisites: An opponent cannot block creatures you control.
Steps: Deal combat damage using the creature that has Breath of Fury attached and Seluma, triggering both Breath of Fury and Seluma.
  Resolve the Seluma trigger, returning an Angel from your graveyard to the battlefield.
  Resolve the Breath of Fury trigger, causing you to sacrifice the creature it is attached to, then attach Breath of Fury to the Angel returned in step 2, then causing you to untap all creatures you control and to get an additional combat phase after this one.
  Repeat each combat phase.
Notes: One of the two additional Angels must be on the battlefield with the other in your graveyard. If both chosen Angels have haste, then Tuktuk Rubblefort can be omitted.

Produces: Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite combat damage; Infinite combat phases; Infinite death triggers; Infinite mana creatures you control can produce; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite untap of creatures you control
Requires: 2x Angel Creature (battlefield, graveyard)
Start: Breath of Fury (battlefield: attached to the other Angel on the battlefield); Seluma, Light of Aysen (battlefield); Kratos, God of War (battlefield)
Prerequisites: An opponent cannot block creatures you control.
Steps: Deal combat damage using the creature that has Breath of Fury attached and Seluma, triggering both Breath of Fury and Seluma.
  Resolve the Seluma trigger, returning an Angel from your graveyard to the battlefield.
  Resolve the Breath of Fury trigger, causing you to sacrifice the creature it is attached to, then attach Breath of Fury to the Angel returned in step 2, then causing you to untap all creatures you control and to get an additional combat phase after this one.
  Repeat each combat phase.
Notes: One of the two additional Angels must be on the battlefield with the other in your graveyard. If both chosen Angels have haste, then Kratos can be omitted.

## Combo 4246-5540-7512
Cards: Zidane, Tantalus Thief + Confusion in the Ranks + Null Rod
Color identity: RW | Bracket: Spicy | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite artifact ETB; Infinite artifact LTB; Infinite artifact sacrifice triggers; Infinite artifact tokens; Infinite Treasure tokens; Infinite Treasure tokens for one or more opponents; Infinite artifact ETB for one or more opponents; Infinite colored mana
Start: Zidane, Tantalus Thief (battlefield); Confusion in the Ranks (battlefield); Null Rod (battlefield)
Prerequisites: You have a way to cause an artifact, creature, or enchantment to enter the battlefield.
  An opponent controls an artifact.
  If you are going to have a nonartifact creature or enchantment enter the battlefield, an opponent must also control either a creature or enchantment respectively.
Steps: Cause an artifact, creature or enchantment to enter the battlefield.
  Confusion in the Ranks triggers, exchanging control of the permanent that entered with an opponent for another permanent of the same type.
  Zidane triggers, creating a Treasure token.
  When the Treasure enters, Confusion in the Ranks triggers, exchanging control of the Treasure with an opponent for another artifact.
  Repeat from step 3.
Notes: Null Rod prevents the opponents from sacrificing the Treasures for mana. Without it, your opponents can sacrifice the Treasures and stop you (eventually) from exchanging control of artifacts.

Produces: Infinite creature LTB; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite combat phases; Infinite untap of creatures you control; Infinite mana creatures you control can produce; Put all Equipment cards from your library onto the battlefield
Start: Mysterious Limousine (battlefield); Godo, Bandit Warlord (battlefield, exile); Urabrask the Hidden (battlefield); Combat Celebrant (battlefield, exile)

  Repeat each combat phase.
